### 2026-11-13 14:49:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Korea #Stock Market #Importance 1

Korean Shares Close Nearly 1% Lower — The benchmark KOSPI fell 0.98% to close at 5,290.53 on Friday, erasing earlier gains as investors turned cautious over the potential impact of Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang’s reported departure on the global AI ecosystem. The index initially jumped 2.05% at the open before reversing course and slipping into negative territory around midday. The shift in sentiment followed overnight losses on Wall Street after news of Huang’s exit, with the Dow Jones, S&P 500, and Nasdaq falling 0.36%, 0.35%, and 0.39%, respectively. Meanwhile, investors remained cautious ahead of US producer price data, which could provide further clues on the Federal Reserve’s monetary policy following a stronger-than-expected consumer inflation reading earlier in the week. Among individual stocks, Samsung Biologics (-0.41%), Naver (-0.33%), and LG Chem (-0.62%) declined, while Samsung Electronics (2.64%) and SK Hynix (2.79%) advanced.

【Tencent Cloud removes tiered pay-as-you-go pricing for MySQL】Tencent Cloud will change MySQL pricing at 16:00 Beijing time on Jan 1, 2027, eliminating duration-based tiered pricing for pay-as-you-go; after the change pay-as-you-go rates will no longer vary by usage duration. The adjustment applies to local-disk MySQL two-node (formerly high-availability), three-node (formerly financial), four-node and read-only instances; cloud-disk instances are not affected.

【高盛預測2027年10年期美債收益率將降至4.6%】高盛集團預測，受避險對沖需求上升、通脹形勢溫和的支撐，到2027年底，美國10年期國債收益率將回落至4.6%，低於市場普遍預期。由倫敦的卡馬克什亞·特里維迪牽頭的策略師團隊在研報中寫道，美國國債當前收益可觀，估值也處於“低位”。但他們補充稱，收益率上行正在引發壓力，說明高位水平難以持續。彭博調查的平均預期顯示，10年期美債收益率2027年底將為4.82%，上週五該收益率報5.12%。分析師寫道：“久期資產的利好邏輯已經轉向。儘管能源價格飆升仍是短期風險，但即便在近期上漲之後，我們依然看好債券配置，部分原因在於其對沖價值。”

### 2026-11-13 14:42:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Iron Ore Slides Toward Four-Year Low — Iron ore futures declined to around CNY 670 per ton, approaching their lowest levels since October 2022 as rising supply and weakening demand continued to weigh on the market. Industry data showed that iron ore inventories at major Chinese ports increased for another week, with Brazilian shipments contributing to the buildup for a fourth consecutive week. Demand prospects were further dampened by anticipated production restrictions across key steelmaking hubs in China's Hebei province. Major steel-producing cities, including Tangshan and Handan, have entered the autumn-winter air pollution control period, during which unfavorable weather conditions have periodically disrupted sintering operations and blast furnace utilization. Meanwhile, industry figures indicated that average hot metal output among Chinese steelmakers, a key gauge of iron ore demand, continued to decline, adding further pressure to prices.

### 2026-11-13 14:40:45  #其他

【軟銀押注AI燃氣輪機失利，計提37億美元減值】軟銀此前訂購了超13吉瓦的發電容量，足以滿足1100萬戶家庭用電。知情人士透露，訂單裡包含大量小型燃氣輪機。相比大型機型，它們交付速度更快，但成本更高、燃油效率更低。知情人士表示，軟銀被迫計提這筆大額減值，直接導致三季度意外錄得70億美元虧損，減值標的主要就是這批小型輪機，原因在於孫正義的採購時機判斷失誤。接近軟銀的人士稱，長期來看，繼續履約持有這批裝置的成本，會高於直接計提減值。知情人士表示，孫正義原本預判這批額外採購的輪機能夠找到足夠需求，但他判斷失準——軟銀的裝置採購承諾遠超自身實際需要。軟銀在給媒體的宣告中稱：“發電裝置規格迭代、能源供給走向多元化。因此，相比我們最初訂購的裝置，當前機型的交付週期、裝置成本與安裝成本都已大幅最佳化。”軟銀補充，其資料中心業務需求超出預期，其他板塊盈利改善，將遠遠覆蓋調整原有訂單帶來的損失

### 2026-11-13 14:30:58  #其他

【金融監管總局法規司司長：討論經濟金融適配性 也應關注金融穩定】11月13日，金融監管總局首席律師、法規司司長王勝邦在第十七屆財新峰會上表示，當前在討論經濟金融適配性時，關注更多的是經濟金融適配對經濟增長的影響，其對金融穩定的影響一定程度上被忽視了。王勝邦指出，經濟與金融是映象關係，相互對映，實體經濟與金融部門之間存在多重複雜的相互作用。經濟金融合理適配，有利於提高金融資源配置效率、促進經濟持續增長，降低金融風險和維護金融穩定。反之，如果經濟金融錯配不僅會拖累經濟增長，而且會導致金融失衡，積累金融風險。他表示，在金融發展初級階段，金融擴張有利於經濟增長和金融穩定，如果金融過度發展可能會拖累經濟增長，進而導致金融風險累積。進入新時代，黨中央反覆強調統籌發展和安全，既要充分發揮金融服務實體經濟發展方面的功能，又強調要加強金融監管、維護金融穩定。此外，“十五五”規劃也明確提出，推動各類金融機構專注主業、完善治理、錯位發展。因此在討論經濟金融適配性時，要同時關注增長和穩定兩個方面。

### 2026-11-13 14:21:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Finland #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Finland Inflation Rises to 3.1% in October — Finland’s annual inflation rate rose to 3.1% in October 2026 from 2.5% in the previous month. It marked the highest reading since January 2024, driven mainly by higher prices for food and non-alcoholic beverages (2.6% vs 1.9% in September), housing and utilities (3% vs 1.6%), and transport (9.6% vs 8.9%). Additional upward pressure came from recreation, sports and culture (1.8% vs 1.5%), furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance (3.1% vs. 2.9%), and alcoholic beverages and tobacco (4.3% vs 3.8%). In contrast, price growth slowed for information and communication (3.4% vs 4.5%), restaurants and accommodation services (1.8% vs 2.0%), and health (2.3% vs 2.5%), while remaining unchanged for education at 4.2%. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose by 0.4%, easing from a 0.7% increase in September.

【Legal director at China's Financial Regulatory Administration: consolidation of small and medium banks is inevitable】China's Financial Regulatory Administration chief lawyer and director of the Legal Affairs Department Wang Shengbang told the Caixin Summit on Nov. 13 that consolidation of small and medium-sized banks is a global trend. He cited US bank legal entities falling from ~12,300 in 1990 to ~3,800 in 2025 (≈69% decline) and Germany's from ~4,500 to ~1,300 (>71% decline). Wang said last year's Central Economic Work Conference directed a reduction in the number of small financial institutions under the principle of "reduce legal entities, not services," using market-based, law-based measures — including absorptions, mergers and restructurings — to lower small-bank legal-entity counts, strengthen banking-system resilience and improve the quality and sustainability of financial supply.

【China regulator legal chief says economic-financial alignment must consider financial stability as well as growth】Nov 13 — Wang Shengbang, chief legal officer and director of the Regulations Department at China’s Financial Regulatory Administration, told the 17th Caixin Summit that discussions of economic-financial alignment have emphasized effects on economic growth while underweighting impacts on financial stability. He said the economy and finance mirror each other with complex interactions: proper alignment improves allocation efficiency, supports sustained growth and reduces financial risk, whereas misalignment can both drag growth and create financial imbalances that accumulate risk. Wang also noted the 15th Five-Year Plan calls for financial institutions to focus on core businesses, improve governance and pursue differentiated development, so assessments of economic‑financial fit should consider growth and stability together.

A survey of economists pegs Q3 GDP growth at 0.9% YoY, down from 1.3% in Q2. The Russian central bank has warned that drone attacks are eroding production capacity across sectors. Rosstat reports refinery output fell 18% YoY in Q3, the largest quarterly decline since 2020; lower fuel output has deepened the mining downturn and pushed wholesale trade into negative growth. Damage to loading docks and port infrastructure has also hit exports. Oxford Economics estimates lost crude and oil-product exports reduced Q3 total export YoY growth by about 3.75 percentage points and subtracted roughly 0.7 percentage points from GDP growth.

### 2026-11-13 13:58:35  #其他

【金融監管總局法規司司長：討論經濟金融適配性 也應關注金融穩定】11月13日，金融監管總局首席律師、法規司司長王勝邦在第十七屆財新峰會上表示，當前在討論經濟金融適配性時，關注更多的是經濟金融適配對經濟增長的影響，其對金融穩定的影響一定程度上被忽視了。王勝邦指出，經濟與金融是映象關係，相互對映，實體經濟與金融部門之間存在多重複雜的相互作用。經濟金融合理適配，有利於提高金融資源配置效率、促進經濟持續增長，降低金融風險和維護金融穩定。反之，如果經濟金融錯配不僅會拖累經濟增長，而且會導致金融失衡，積累金融風險。他表示，在金融發展初級階段，金融擴張有利於經濟增長和金融穩定，如果金融過度發展可能會拖累經濟增長，進而導致金融風險累積。進入新時代，黨中央反覆強調統籌發展與安全，既要充分發揮金融服務實體經濟發展方面的功能，又強調要加強金融監管、維護金融穩定。此外，“十五五”規劃也明確提出，推動各類金融機構專注主業、完善治理、錯位發展。因此在討論經濟金融適配性時，要同時關注增長和穩定兩個方面。

NZX 50 Ends 0.6% Lower but Logs 3rd Weekly Gain — The NZX 50 shed 86 points, or 0.6%, to 14,174 on Friday, as traders took profits after the index hit a record high a day earlier, supported by strong earnings reports from heavyweight stocks. Rising oil prices pressured the index, as inflation concerns and expectations of interest rate hikes increased. Fresh data also pressured sentiment, as growth in New Zealand's manufacturing sector eased in October amid rising fuel prices, the cost of living, and concerns over election uncertainty. Caution also built ahead of the release of a slew of economic data from China, including industrial output, as well as next week's PBOC interest rate decision, FOMC minutes, and New Zealand's trade data. Almost all sectors traded in the red, led by utilities, with notable losers including AFT Pharmaceuticals (-3.2%), Fletcher Building (-2.4%), Meridian Energy (-2.1%), Delegat Group (-1.4%), and Contact Energy (-1.2%). However, the index rose 1.3% for the week, marking its third straight weekly gain.

Sensex Dips After 11-Day Winning Streak — India’s BSE Sensex edged down about 0.2% to 81,739 on Friday, snapping an eleven-session winning streak as rising crude oil prices and inflation concerns offset strong foreign fund inflows. Meanwhile, India’s retail inflation accelerated to 6.09% in October from 5.54% in September, breaching the RBI’s 6% upper tolerance threshold for the first time since October 2024. Global concerns over heavy data centre investments also weighed on AI stocks, adding to market uncertainty. Among stocks, Ola Electric fell 2.7% despite a narrower second-quarter loss, as revenue dropped 39%. Vodafone declined 2.1% after reporting a narrower quarterly loss on higher average revenue per user, while Meesho gained 0.7% amid reports of a potential 1% stake sale by a SoftBank affiliate through a block deal worth approximately 11.74 billion rupees.

Rupiah Recovers but Heads for Weekly Losses — The Indonesian rupiah appreciated, trading near IDR 18,200 per U.S. dollar on Friday after recent losses brought it closer to its record low of IDR 18,279. Sentiment improved after a Bank Indonesia official said the central bank would remain active in the market to support currency stability and ensure the rupiah reflects its fundamentals ahead of next week's policy meeting. Government officials also reassured investors that economic activity remained normal despite Vice President Gibran Rakabuming's resignation this week. However, the rupiah is on track to lose around 1.1% for the week, reversing prior gains. Pressure stemmed partly from expected fund outflows of around IDR 1.2 trillion ahead of the Nov. 30 implementation of MSCI's latest review, which removed two Indonesian constituents while retaining seven others. Globally, the dollar held near a 19-month high, lifted by hotter-than-expected U.S. inflation and hawkish Fed remarks that reinforced views of tighter monetary policy.

### 2026-11-13 12:46:06  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Currency #Importance 1

Rupee Retreats From Over 6-Month High — The Indian rupee weakened to around 94.15 per dollar, pulling back from over six-month highs as a rebound in crude oil prices renewed pressure on the currency. Brent crude climbed toward $96 per barrel following fresh attacks on Saudi energy infrastructure and disruptions to shipping through the Strait of Hormuz, reviving concerns over India’s oil import bill and demand for the dollar. At the same time, higher US Treasury yields supported the dollar, adding to external pressure on the currency by reducing the relative attractiveness of emerging-market assets. Moreover, speculation that the Reserve Bank of India is purchasing dollars to rebuild its foreign exchange reserves, alongside renewed spot-market demand from oil companies, may be absorbing additional dollar supply and limiting the rupee’s recovery despite robust foreign equity inflows. Nevertheless, sustained foreign investment inflows continue to cushion external financing pressures.

Korean Won Extends Gains on Chipmaker Dollar Sales — The South Korean won strengthened to around 1,330 per dollar, extending gains to its strongest level since late October, as steady dollar selling by the country’s semiconductor exporters helped cap upward pressure on the exchange rate. Chipmakers have remained consistent in converting their foreign-currency earnings, providing a key source of support for the won. However, further gains were limited by rising global oil prices and US Treasury yields, which bolstered the dollar amid fading hopes for US-Iran negotiations and increased expectations of a December Federal Reserve rate hike. Meanwhile, Bank of Korea data showed that import prices fell 1.8% month-on-month in October, reversing a 1.9% increase in September, largely reflecting a 1.6% appreciation in the won against the dollar and a 2.6% decline in Dubai crude prices.

【環球時報快評：黃仁勳剛辭職，美媒就臆想“中必輸”】黃仁勳前腳剛從英偉達辭職，美國彭博社後腳就立刻發表文章，宣稱中國成了“最大輸家”。文章邏輯極為奇葩：由於中國AI科技公司的技術優勢，都是靠“走私美國晶片”和“蒸餾美國技術”實現的，作為美國對華晶片限制的“漏洞”、矽谷開源模式最有力的倡導者之一，黃仁勳倒臺後，中美AI合作的希望“被徹底澆滅”，中國將被孤立在世界的“另一邊”。 彭博社似乎早就準備好了這篇文章，就等著黃仁勳辭職，好把這頂帽子給中國扣過來。這些年，所謂“中國最大輸家”的說法在美國媒體上不勝其數，從美國次貸危機到美伊衝突、日元貶值，出點什麼事就總有人扯上“中國又輸了”，以至於中國網友造出了一個專門諷刺這種現象的詞：“中必輸”。 這種說法在現實中幾乎出一次就被打一次臉。在複雜的國際形勢中，它把各種錯綜複雜的關係簡化為純粹的輸贏遊戲，任何事件都優先解讀為“中國受損”，試圖以此維持“美國例外”的心理優勢，逃避自身面臨重重危機的現實。屢屢“預測”屢屢失敗，不僅削弱了自身的認知能力，這些年來也對美國對外政策形成一定誤導，使美國付出了更高的戰略代價。

### 2026-11-13 12:11:59  #其他

【世衛：2025年全球結核病發病和死亡人數繼續下降】世界衛生組織12日釋出的《2026年全球結核病報告》顯示，2025年全球估計有1060萬人罹患結核病，110萬人死於結核病，發病和死亡人數繼續下降。但國際融資下降、診斷缺口和區域進展不平衡仍對結核病防治構成挑戰。報告顯示，2015年以來，全球結核病死亡人數下降了35%，自2000年以來，及時治療已挽救約8600萬人的生命。但報告認為進展仍然太慢，2015年以來全球結核病發病率僅下降14%，遠未達到到2025年下降50%的里程碑目標。結核病仍是單一傳染源導致死亡的首要原因。報告還顯示，全球結核病負擔仍然高度集中在30個國家，它們佔2025年結核病新增病例的88%。

Malaysia Retail Sales Growth Hits 4-Month High — Retail sales in Malaysia increased 6.9% year-on-year in September 2026, following a 6.5% rise in August, marking the strongest growth since May. Sales growth was driven mainly by non-specialised stores (7.9% vs. 7.5%), other household equipment in specialised stores (3.8% vs 3.5%), automotive fuel in specialised stores (10.6% vs 8.9%), information and communication equipment in specialised stores (6.8% vs 4.0%), and food, beverages and tobacco in specialised stores (6.3% vs 6.1%). Meanwhile, sales of other goods in specialised stores eased to 6.0% from 6.9%. On a monthly basis, retail sales rose 2.7%, following a 0.5% gain in August, marking the fastest increase in six months. Meanwhile, wholesale trade growth accelerated to 25.3% from 16.6% in August, while motor vehicle sales growth rose to 13.4% from 10.5%.

【市場監管總局等三部門聯合印發通知 深化拓展企業資訊變更等四項“一件事”】近日，市場監管總局、住房城鄉建設部、國家醫保局聯合印發《關於拓展企業資訊變更等四項“一件事”辦理事項的通知》，聚焦經營主體全生命週期高頻辦事需求，在前期實現企業資訊變更、企業登出登記、個體工商戶轉型為企業、企業遷移登記等“一件事”整合辦理基礎上，進一步拓展關聯辦理事項，實現“一次申請、一窗受理、一網通辦、一次辦結”，持續最佳化營商環境，增強經營主體獲得感。拓展企業資訊變更“一件事”，聯動全鏈條資訊變更。將“基本醫療保險單位參保登記資訊變更”拓展納入企業資訊變更“一件事”。企業在市場監管部門依法辦理變更登記後，市場監管部門依託“一網通辦”等政務服務平臺共享變更資訊，醫保部門接收資訊後，對應完成基本醫療保險單位參保登記資訊變更。拓展企業登出登記“一件事”，實現登出“一網”服務。將“住房公積金單位繳存登記登出”“建設工程企業資質登出登記”“基本醫療保險單位參保登出登記”拓展納入企業登出登記“一件事”。對無住房公積金欠繳和醫保欠費等情形的企業，住房城鄉建設部門、醫保部門根據登出平臺共享的資訊辦理住房公積金單位繳存登記登出、建設工程企業資質登出登記（不含吸收合併類登出事項）和參保登出登記。三部門要求，各地市場監管部門要切實履行牽頭職責，會同住房城鄉建設、醫保等部門做好事項梳理、流程最佳化、系統對接、指南編制，強化系統聯動和業務流程再造，做好政策宣傳解讀與視窗培訓，推動四項“一件事”拓展辦理落地見效。

### 2026-11-13 12:03:11  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Japan 10-Year Yield Hits Three-Week High — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield rose to around 2.87% on Friday, reaching its highest level in three weeks and following the upward movement in US Treasury yields amid growing expectations of a more aggressive Federal Reserve tightening cycle. The increase followed stronger-than-expected US consumer inflation data and hawkish remarks from Fed officials. Meanwhile, the Bank of Japan is widely expected to raise interest rates next month and continue tightening monetary policy into next year. The BOJ’s latest Summary of Opinions revealed broad support among policymakers for further rate increases, as underlying inflation has reached levels generally consistent with its 2% price stability target. However, board member Toichiro Asada called for caution regarding the timing of additional hikes, pointing out that household consumption remains subdued and recent price increases have not necessarily been driven by strong demand.

### 2026-11-13 11:59:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Palm Oil Set for First Weekly Advance in Three Weeks — Malaysian palm oil futures held recent gains, hovering near MYR 4,500 per tonne, supported by a weaker ringgit and firmer edible oils on the Dalian markets. Sentiment also benefited from a smaller-than-expected stock build, with October inventories reaching a record 3.62 million metric tons, but below market estimates of 3.75 million tons. Meanwhile, seasonal production declines across Southeast Asia are expected as oil palm trees enter their lower-yield period in November. However, gains were limited by expectations of softer demand, with cargo surveyors reporting a 1.9–2.3% decline in Malaysian palm oil shipments during November 1–10 from the same period in October. Lower crude oil prices also weighed on risk appetite, reducing palm oil's appeal as a biodiesel feedstock. Separately, MPOB monthly data showed production rose for a fifth consecutive month in October. Despite these headwinds, futures are up around 4.5% this week, heading for their first weekly gain in three weeks.

Corn Struggles to Extend Rebound — Corn futures traded around $4.4 per bushel, struggling to sustain their rebound from a three-month low as a stronger US dollar and favorable South American weather offset tighter domestic supply projections. The USDA's November WASDE report lowered projected US corn production to 15.89 billion bushels from 16.03 billion in October, while ending stocks were revised down to 1.758 billion bushels from 1.849 billion. Meanwhile, the US harvest reached 81% completion as of November 8, five percentage points behind the five-year average, with wet conditions slowing fieldwork in major producing states. However, export demand remained a concern, with US corn export inspections running nearly 16% below last year's pace. A stronger dollar, which climbed to a 19-month high following hotter-than-expected US inflation, further pressured prices by making American supplies less competitive overseas. Traders now await the USDA's weekly export sales report for fresh demand signals.

【矽谷科技領袖批評英偉達急讓CEO去職：不排除延攬黃仁勳、期望王者歸來】英偉達執行長去職在矽谷引起軒然大波，眾多創投家和企業創始人紛紛批評這家晶片製造商的決定，並擔憂該決定對整個產業的深遠影響。 “黑暗、黑暗的一天，”Andreessen Horowitz普通合夥人Martin Casado在X發文說。“對美國和西方世界來說，這是個爛決定。”人力資源軟體新創公司Deel執行長Alex Bouaziz稱英偉達此舉是“一個錯誤”。區塊鏈公司Solana Labs Inc.聯席創始人Raj Gokal稱這是“世代級的失誤”。Runway AI Inc.聯席執行長Cristóbal Valenzuela和Conviction創始人Sarah Guo等人則稱之為“巨大的損失”。 一些科技領袖甚至表示，未來不排除會聘請黃仁勳。Casado表示，如果黃仁勳能夠加入Andreessen Horowitz，將是“一項殊榮”。SpaceX執行長馬斯克週三的貼文也有類似措辭，當時黃仁勳尚未辭職。有些人還抱持希望，認為黃仁勳有朝一日可能重返英偉達掌舵，就像賈伯斯和Altman當年的經歷一樣。“三、四年後，英偉達的財報表現若連續一兩季不如預期，他們就會把黃仁勳請回來帶領公司，”推特前執行長Dick Costolo說。“如同名氣一樣，所有人的怒氣頂多維持十五分鐘，而且美國人最愛的就是東山再起的故事。”

The 10-year JGB yield rose 8bps to 2.87%; the 30-year yield climbed 7bps to 3.99%. Market pricing for Bank of Japan tightening firmed: OIS imply roughly an 87% chance of a rate increase before December. Tokai Tokyo Securities chief strategist Kazuhiko Sano said the JGB and U.S. Treasury selloff could be an overreaction but there is no clear catalyst for a sustained JGB rally; any rebound is likely to be limited.

### 2026-11-13 11:27:54  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Soybeans Trade Near 2-Week Highs — Soybean futures traded above $12.2 per bushel, hovering near their highest level in two weeks, supported by South American planting risks and tightening global stock projections. The November 10 WASDE report raised US soybean production to 4.574 billion bushels but lowered ending stocks to 300 million bushels, as higher domestic use and exports more than offset the production increase. Global ending stocks were also revised down to 123.5 million metric tons. Elsewhere, heavy El Niño-related rainfall is forecast across Argentina, Paraguay and southern Brazil in the second half of November. Excessive rain could delay soybean planting and introduce uncertainty over the 2027 crop. Meanwhile, China has reportedly purchased 21.8 million metric tons of US soybeans so far in 2026, equivalent to 87% of its 25-million-ton annual commitment. However, competitively priced Brazilian supplies remain a major source of competition.

### 2026-11-13 11:22:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #News #Importance 1

Indonesia Posts Budget Deficit of 1.47% of GDP in Jan-Oct — Indonesia posted a budget deficit of IDR 379.3 trillion (USD 20.81 billion) in the first 10 months of 2026, equivalent to 1.47% of GDP, Finance Minister Suahasil Nazara said on Friday. During the period, revenues reached IDR 2,618 trillion, up 23.9% year-on-year, while fiscal spending rose 15.6% to IDR 2,997 trillion. The latest figure was lower than in the same period last year, when the budget registered a deficit of IDR 479.7 trillion, or 2.02% of GDP. He added that, for the full fiscal year 2026, the budget is on track to post a deficit of 2.85% of GDP, unchanged from the government's projection. Meanwhile, the primary balance of the 2026 state budget recorded a surplus of IDR 114.8 trillion as of October 2026. This figure was higher than the primary balance in October 2025, when the government recorded a deficit of IDR 45 trillion.

### 2026-11-13 11:01:25  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

European Natural Gas Prices Climb to Two-Week High — European natural gas prices climbed above €95/MWh, hitting a two-week high, underpinned by curtailed LNG exports from the Middle East, colder weather expectations, and disruptions to Norwegian supplies. Supply risks remained elevated amid disruptions to LNG shipments through the Strait of Hormuz, forcing Europe to rely more heavily on alternative suppliers and compete with Asian buyers for available cargoes. Meanwhile, colder conditions are forecast across parts of Europe from November 17, with further cold spells possible toward late November and early December, likely driving up heating demand as the region enters winter. Norwegian gas deliveries to Europe were around 9% below year-earlier levels on November 11, partly due to unplanned outages at the Troll and Åsgard facilities. EU gas storage facilities were less than 72% full, compared with a five-year seasonal average of around 89%, leaving the region with a relatively thin supply buffer. Germany’s storage levels were below 60%.

Indonesian Stocks Rise to 2-Week High, Eye Solid Weekly Gain — Indonesian equities rose 39 points, or 0.8%, to 5,291 in early Friday trading, extending gains to a two-week high. Markets are on track for a strong weekly advance of around 5.3%, boosted by easing political uncertainty after Vice President Gibran Rakabuming resigned, with government officials reassuring investors that economic activity remains unaffected. Meanwhile, MSCI's November review removed Barito Pacific and Bumi Resources from its global index, leaving just seven Indonesian constituents, including the four major banks. Nearly all sectors moved higher, led by tech, property, energy, and financials. However, gains were tempered by caution ahead of next week's Bank Indonesia meeting, as policymakers face a growing trade-off between rupiah stability and economic growth. Globally, Wall Street ended lower overnight following Nvidia’s leadership shakeup. Top performers included ESSA Industries (9.6%), Solusi Sinergi Digital (7.4%), Semen Indonesia (3.9%), and Telkom Indonesia (2.3%).

US 10-Year Yield Holds at Three-Week High — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note held above 5.1% on Friday, hovering at three-week highs amid growing expectations for a faster pace of Federal Reserve policy tightening. The upward move followed hotter-than-expected US consumer inflation data and hawkish remarks from Fed officials, with some policymakers suggesting that a larger 50-basis-point rate increase may be needed to bring inflation under control. Markets are currently pricing in around an 85% probability of a 25-basis-point rate hike in December, while the likelihood of a larger 50-basis-point increase stands at around 15%. Investors are also increasingly anticipating further rate hikes next year. Market participants are now awaiting US PPI data for additional insights into inflationary pressures. Meanwhile, oil prices rebounded as President Donald Trump declared that the US no longer needs a deal with Iran, adding to upward pressure on Treasury yields.

Dollar Holds at 19-Month High — The US dollar index held above 103 on Friday after gaining for three consecutive sessions and reaching its highest level since April 2025, supported by rising expectations of a more aggressive Federal Reserve tightening cycle. The rally followed stronger-than-expected US consumer inflation data and hawkish comments from Fed officials, with some policymakers indicating that a larger 50-basis-point rate increase could be necessary to bring inflation under control. Markets are currently assigning an approximately 85% probability to a 25-basis-point rate hike in December, while the likelihood of a larger 50-basis-point increase stands at around 15%. Traders are also increasingly factoring in additional rate hikes next year. Investors are now turning their attention to US PPI data for further indications of inflationary trends. Meanwhile, oil prices rebounded as President Donald Trump declared that the US no longer needs a deal with Iran, boosting safe-haven demand for the dollar.

【段永平回應黃仁勳辭職】段永平透過賬號“大道無形我有型”回覆網友提問。面對網友詢問黃仁勳辭職對英偉達的衝擊，段永平寫道：“大概這是董事會最後的決定了。短期對英偉達的生意不會有影響，幾年後就要看他們能不能找到好的CEO了。黃仁勳是個工作非常努力的人，同時對AI的前景也很有遠見，要找到這樣的人很難。” 在另一條回覆中，段永平稱英偉達是那種可以“傻瓜式管理”的公司。“英偉達的商業模式是需要有很好的管理團隊的，不然3-5年後可能就沒那麼厲害了。不過，AI才剛剛開始，估計未來十年都會有很好的成長。” 他還補充了對黃仁勳迴歸可能性的判斷，認為如果當初促使黃仁勳辭職的核心原因沒有消除，他大機率不會重新回到崗位，但是存在一種可能性，就是公司邀請他擔任顧問，成為類似“影子CEO”的角色，保留影響力，只是不再擁有簽字決策權，而這個方向，要等到聽證會結束之後才有可能出現明確結論。

China Stocks Waver as Trump-Xi Summit Approaches — The Shanghai Composite rose 0.2% to 3,663 on Friday, while the Shenzhen Component edged down 0.1% to 11,186, delivering a mixed performance as investors adopted a cautious stance ahead of next week’s summit between US President Donald Trump and Chinese President Xi Jinping. The high-stakes meeting in Shenzhen faces renewed uncertainty after allegations surrounding Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang’s ties to a suspected Chinese spy threatened to reignite bilateral friction over semiconductor trade. Huang’s controversy could hand US lawmakers fresh ammunition to push for stricter technology controls ahead of the leaders’ talks. Notable gainers included Cambricon Technologies (4.7%) and Hygon Information Technology (3.8%), while optical module suppliers Zhongji Innolight (-3.2%) and Eoptolink Technology (-2.9%) retreated. Despite the day’s divergent moves, both benchmark indices remained on track to post weekly gains.

Hong Kong Stocks Extend Losing Streak on Tech Concerns — The Hang Seng Index fell 0.4% to 23,917 on Friday, extending losses for a third consecutive session as concerns over Nvidia's leadership turmoil and its implications for China's technology sector weighed on sentiment, despite a recovery in regional semiconductor shares. Nvidia's leadership scandal revived concerns over tighter US export restrictions and potential disruptions to advanced chip supplies for Chinese AI developers. The Hang Seng Tech Index remained up about 5% in November but lost momentum amid concerns over rising AI investment costs and profitability. In addition, Chinese AI satellite maker Adaspace Technology slipped 1.8% in its Hong Kong trading debut after raising HK$750 million through its IPO. Investors also awaited Hong Kong's third-quarter GDP figures and looked ahead to earnings from Tencent, Alibaba, Baidu and JD.com next week. Notable laggards included Tencent (-2.0%), J&T (-1.5%) and Xiaomi (-1.0%).

Silver Heads for Weekly Loss — Silver prices recovered above $50 per ounce on Friday after declining in the previous session, but remained on track for a weekly loss as a stronger US dollar and rising Treasury yields reflected growing expectations of more aggressive Federal Reserve rate hikes. The shift in market sentiment followed hotter-than-expected US consumer inflation data and hawkish comments from Fed officials, with some policymakers suggesting that a larger 50-basis-point rate increase may be necessary to contain inflation. Markets are currently pricing in around an 85% probability of a 25 bps rate hike in December, as well as a 15% chance of a larger 50 bps increase. Traders are also increasingly anticipating additional rate hikes next year. Meanwhile, gold faced further downward pressure from recovering oil prices after President Donald Trump stated that the US no longer needs a deal with Iran.

【比亞迪：打擊網路黑公關亂象。符合條件的線索提供者給予50萬元至500萬元獎勵】比亞迪法務部微博發文：為捍衛企業正當權益，維護清朗的網路空間與公平競爭的市場秩序，我司持續推進網路侵權維權工作，現將部分維權案件進展公示如下： 馮某、季某惡意編造涉比亞迪不實資訊並在社交平臺散佈。比亞迪報警後，公安機關迅速展開調查並抓獲二人，認定二人的行為屬於擾亂公共秩序的違法行為，已依法對二人予以拘留處罰。 姜某在抖音平臺長期、多次釋出侮辱、詆譭比亞迪的言論，嚴重損害比亞迪品牌聲譽。比亞迪報警後，公安機關認定其行為違法，依法對其予以拘留處罰。 網路賬號“娛樂**”“車界**”“四五**”“**司機”等，長期針對比亞迪品牌及產品釋出詆譭性言論，比亞迪起訴後，經法院審理認定構成侵權，判令相關主體公開道歉並賠償經濟損失。 此外，我們注意到，部分MCN機構旗下網路賬號，集中釋出針對比亞迪的文章。經初步核查，發現網路賬號所屬的MCN機構有**涅**、**遠**、**馬**、**牛**、**洲**等數十家，其中部分網路賬號藉助AI生成標題雷同、行文相似的同質化文章，並以“闢謠式傳謠”等方式惡意釋出，誤導公眾認知，嚴重損害比亞迪品牌聲譽。我們已對相關內容完成證據保全，正採取法律手段追究相關主體的責任。 同時，為打擊網路黑公關亂象，對於透過MCN等有組織、有策劃地抹黑比亞迪的相關線索，一經查實，將對符合條件的線索提供者給予50萬元至500萬元獎勵；其他符合條件的線索提供者，給予5萬元至500萬元獎勵。

Gold Set for Weekly Drop — Gold prices recovered above $3,900 per ounce on Friday after declining for three consecutive sessions, but remained on track for a sharp weekly loss as a stronger US dollar and rising Treasury yields reflected growing expectations of more aggressive Federal Reserve rate hikes. The shift in market sentiment followed hotter-than-expected US consumer inflation data and hawkish comments from Fed officials, with some policymakers suggesting that a larger 50-basis-point rate increase may be necessary to contain inflation. Markets are currently pricing in around an 85% probability of a 25 bps rate hike in December, as well as a 15% chance of a larger 50 bps increase. Traders are also increasingly anticipating additional rate hikes next year. Meanwhile, gold faced further downward pressure from recovering oil prices after President Donald Trump stated that the US no longer needs a deal with Iran.

### 2026-11-13 09:53:06  #觀點 #央行 #其他

【央行鄒瀾：信貸降速提質成宏觀執行新常態 徵信體系適配性有待提升】11月13日，中國人民銀行副行長鄒瀾在第十七屆財新峰會上談及我國徵信業的發展與變革。他指出，在金融強國建設、數字中國建設、社會信用建設加速推進的時代背景下，徵信的功能定位、治理邊界、執行邏輯也在隨之調整。鄒瀾表示，經過多年發展，我國金融市場特別是信貸市場結構與層次已發生深刻變化。從總量看，經濟的貨幣化程序基本結束，我國已建成全球最大的銀行體系和世界領先的普惠金融體系，貸款規模接近300萬億，徵信系統中有信貸記錄的人已達8億多，除去老人小孩，絕大多數人都有過信貸經歷。鄒瀾指出，當前信貸降速提質成為宏觀執行新常態，金融發展的重心，從增量擴張轉向存量最佳化，從規模導向轉向精準滴灌。科技創新、綠色轉型、民營小微等是當前金融服務的重點領域和薄弱環節，相應的信用評價、徵信機制和風控模型等信用服務尚不健全，徵信體系適配性有待提升。與此同時，數字金融發展需要大幅拓展徵信資料來源和功能，需要徵信業從專注於傳統、靜態的信貸資訊共享，進化為資料多元、動態互動、技術獨特的綜合性金融資料服務。此外，社會民生信用需求變化也是需要關注的重要變數。

Other major gainers included Shinhan Financial (5.41%), KB Financial (3.89%), and Hyundai Motor (2.93%). Heating Oil Holds Gains — US heating oil futures held around $4.28 per gallon after rebounding from their lowest level since early August, as markets weighed persistent geopolitical risks against signs of improving US inventories. President Donald Trump signaled the US could abandon efforts to reach a deal with Iran, dimming hopes for a diplomatic resolution. He also redirected Vice President JD Vance toward domestic priorities, casting doubt on ongoing backchannel talks. Meanwhile, continued attacks on Middle East shipping and energy infrastructure, alongside intensified Ukrainian strikes on Russian energy facilities, kept concerns over global supply disruptions elevated. However, EIA data showed US distillate inventories, including diesel and heating oil, rose by 4.1 million barrels in the week ended November 6, the largest weekly build since July. The increase offered some relief on the supply front, although stocks remained 8% below the five-year average.

【精品茅臺零售均價領跌 國窖1573止跌反彈 古井貢古20、青花郎漲幅居先】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，11月13日中國白酒業12大單品的終端零售均價七漲三跌兩平，上漲品種的數量明顯勝出，累計漲幅也顯著超越下跌端，整體價盤在經歷三連跌後反彈。上漲端，五糧液普五八代上漲3元至789元，三連陽逼近790元一線，過去六天其價格有五天上漲，觸底反彈走勢明確；五糧液1618上漲3元至820元，連續兩天上漲且漲幅擴大；國窖1573上漲7元至859元，完全收復昨日失地，自過去一個月來最低點強勁反彈；洋河夢之藍M6+上漲4元至589元，連續第二天大幅上揚；古井貢古20上漲6元至516元，昨日平盤蓄力後發力上攻；習酒君品上漲1元至613元，連續第二天小幅上漲，低位有序整理；青花郎上漲6元至659元，終結三連陰頹勢，昨日該酒品零售均價重跌10元之多。下跌端，飛天茅臺的零售均價小跌1元至1765元，連續第四天下跌；精品茅臺下跌5元至2415元，四連陰再創近30天新低，i茅臺開始全天動態補貨模式投放吸引其市價下行；茅臺1935下跌1元至684元，結束過去兩天平盤走勢。持平方面，青花汾20仍為378元，連續第二天維持在這一水平；水晶劍南春平盤於400元關口，近期其價格多次穿行這一整百點位。

今日12大單品的終端零售總價大幅反彈，國窖1573、古井貢古20、青花郎的漲幅強勁，推動整體價盤止跌回升。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10487元，較昨日大幅上漲23元，創下3天以來新高。

【汽油價格高企 但美國人駕車出行熱情未減】根據彭博新能源財經的分析，自伊朗戰爭導致美國汽油價格飆升以來，美國人駕車出行幾乎沒有減少。經通脹調整後的汽油零售價格在5月達到峰值，比戰前的12月至2月平均水平高出53%，超過2008年油價飆升、2011年利比亞內戰以及2022年俄烏戰爭所致漲幅。然而，經季節性因素調整後，5月車輛行駛里程僅比戰前水平下降0.3%，並在6月回升。與以往衝擊相比，一個重要的區別在於消費者的汽油成本負擔。儘管汽油價格上漲，但3月至8月汽油支出僅佔可支配收入的約2.3%，約為2008年和2011年水平的一半。負擔減輕，加上勞動力市場更為強勁，或許可以解釋為何駕車出行依然保持穩定。

### 2026-11-13 08:53:56  #央行 #資料 #市場 #國際

【中信期貨：美元與利率形成雙重壓制，黃金配置需求尚難扭轉調整】黃金連續第三個交易日下跌。美國兩年期國債收益率本週累計上行約20個基點，美元指數在週二、週三累計上漲近1%，持有成本上升與美元走強共同壓制金價，前期反彈未能延續。 美國10月CPI與核心CPI環比分別上漲0.4%和0.3%，均高於預期，核心價格壓力持續成為政策收緊的依據。古爾斯比對通脹走勢表達明顯擔憂，疊加沃勒此前提出更大幅度加息的可能性，市場已充分計入12月加息25個基點，並開始交易加息50個基點的風險。油價回落雖有助於緩解能源通脹，但尚未消除核心通脹黏性，短期難以推動緊縮預期明顯降溫。 配置需求仍為黃金提供支撐。部分黃金ETF恢復增持，央行9月購金亦有所加快，表明價格回落後配置資金開始入場。但投機資金迴流缺乏持續性，官方購金和ETF買盤尚難完全抵消美元、利率上行帶來的壓力。黃金跌破4000美元/盎司，也說明配置需求對價格的支撐仍會受到宏觀環境變化的考驗。（中信期貨）

Australian Equities Slip Further but Heads for Third Weekly Gain — Australian stocks fell 73 points, or 0.8%, to 9,074 in Friday morning trade, extending prior losses amid broad-based declines, led by logistics, tech, and non-energy minerals. Caution prevailed ahead of next week's October labor data after unemployment stayed near a five-year high in September as employment eased. Higher borrowing costs in both the U.S. and Australia also dampened appetite for risk assets. A negative lead from Wall Street overnight added to the pressure after the fallout from Nvidia’s leadership upheaval. SGH Ltd. (-3.2%), Codan Ltd. (-2.5%), and Qantas Airways (-1.9%) were among major laggards. The big four banks lost 0.1–0.6%, while BHP Group slipped 0.7% on weaker iron ore prices and a stronger U.S. dollar. 【CITIC Securities: Tech leadership changes could amplify short-term market volatility but systemic sell-off risk likely limited】CITIC Securities said NVIDIA CEO Jensen Huang’s departure, combined with shifts in the US political backdrop, could trigger a valuation repricing across the AI supply chain and a style rebalance in US equities, though the medium‑term effect depends on corporate earnings evolution. In tech, markets may short‑term reprice slower AI capex growth and downward revisions to semiconductor earnings; supply‑chain inventory risk appears limited and hardware downside should be manageable. Slower AI capex would improve cloud service providers’ (CSPs’) free cash flow, and, together with a potential retreat in long‑end yields, could support the SaaS sector. For US equities, the midterm elections and tech leadership turnover may increase near‑term volatility but systemic selling pressure is likely limited; as political risk is digested, pricing should refocus on earnings growth and upward revisions to underpin medium‑term performance.

【銀河證券：電子行業具備超跌反彈的基本面基礎 關注AI超跌修復視窗期】中國銀河證券研報釋出11月電子行業展望與配置思路。電子行業具備超跌反彈的基本面基礎，關注AI超跌修復視窗期。進入11月，市場重心將從題材預期轉向真實業績兌現，優先選擇三季報已經驗證盈利、四季度景氣延續、估值匹配的標的，關注三條主線：（1）儲存與先進封裝：算力拉動儲存緊俏，原廠長協鎖定遠期產能，先進封裝是算力晶片關鍵瓶頸。重點跟蹤儲存合約價格、大廠擴產、工藝良率。（2）AI-PCB/CCL/MLCC：三季報盈利兌現充分，業績和估值回撥形成反差，建議跟蹤上游原材料緊缺程度、產品交期、海外雲廠商資本開支等。（3）國產裝置、材料與算力晶片：政策持續加持，晶圓廠擴產帶動國產化落地，前期回撥消化估值，關注招標落地進度，留意外部出口管制不確定性。

【經濟日報金觀平：延伸產業鏈實現算力“價值輸出”】當前，我國區域發展不平衡問題依然突出，西部地區迫切需要找到能夠突破地理和自然條件約束、融入全球高階產業分工的發展新路徑。算力建設為打破傳統產業分工格局、縮小東西部發展差距提供了重要契機。實施“東數西算”工程、構建全國一體化算力網，是著眼於促進區域經濟協調發展的重要舉措，有助於破解東部算力需求旺盛、西部資源富集之間的結構性錯配，拓寬東部地區發展空間，更為西部開闢產業發展新賽道，使經濟發展模式從傳統的“賣資源”轉向“賣算力”，推動形成東西聯動、協同發展的格局。這種分工深化與協同合作，將帶動整體產業鏈科技水平的提高，以及國際競爭力的提升。

US Stock Futures Climb as Nvidia Shares Stabilize — US stock futures moved higher on Friday as selling pressure on Nvidia shares eased following the resignation of CEO Jensen Huang amid an ongoing scandal. Futures tied to the Dow Jones, S&P 500, and Nasdaq 100 gained between 0.2% and 0.6%. In after-hours trading, Nvidia shares advanced more than 1%, rebounding from Thursday’s losses as investors evaluated the potential leadership transition at the chipmaker, with CFO Colette Kress assuming the role of interim CEO. Wall Street’s major indexes ended lower on Thursday, with the Dow falling 0.36%, while the S&P 500 and Nasdaq Composite declined 0.35% and 0.39%, respectively. Investors are now looking ahead to October’s PPI report for further indications of inflationary pressures. A stronger-than-expected consumer inflation reading, coupled with hawkish comments from Federal Reserve officials, has reinforced expectations that interest rates could be raised more aggressively.

【中信證券：產業鏈盈利逐步修復，鋰電板塊投資價值凸顯】中信證券研報稱，從電池與能源管理產業鏈2026年前三季度及2026年第三季度經營結果來看，全球儲能需求保持高景氣，新能源汽車需求有所分化，產業鏈多數環節收入實現增長，中上游盈利同比明顯修復，但第三季度環比有所承壓。盈利端，電池環節收入維持較快增長，碳酸鋰、正極、隔膜、電解液及六氟、銅箔&鋁箔等環節盈利同比明顯改善，但部分環節第三季度盈利環比回落。供給端，行業“固定資產+在建工程”同比增速持續回升，2026年第三季度升至20%，同時資本開支呈現明顯分化，電池、正極、負極、結構件等環節同比較快增長，行業逐步由全面收縮轉向結構性擴產。考慮到中國鋰電產業鏈全球競爭優勢顯著、海外佈局逐步完善，同時固態、快充等新技術持續迭代，中信證券認為頭部企業有望憑藉技術、成本及全球化優勢進一步擴大份額和盈利優勢，持續看好產業鏈各環節優質頭部公司。

【華泰證券：AI產業內部或沿回報驗證分化】華泰證券研報稱，AI產業內部或沿回報驗證分化。過去兩週，海外AI資產調整加劇，融資與投入回報壓力繼續傳導，美國中期選舉後的資料中心監管升溫進一步影響專案建設節奏預期。硬體財報仍在兌現需求增長，市場對AI投資的關注則更多落在交付確定性、盈利質量和現金回收。配置上，關注兩類機會：一是訂單、交付與利潤能夠交叉驗證的關鍵基礎設施供應商，包括PCB、光互連等環節，跟蹤合同定價、產品結構和擴產節奏判斷盈利彈性；二是已有收入和現金流驗證的應用平臺，以及端側/物理AI等。此外，建議對依賴新增審批、持續外部融資或遠期利潤假設的公司維持謹慎，配置依據進一步落到增長質量與資金使用效率。

Brent Holds Advance as Trump Downplays Iran Deal — Brent crude prices hovered near $96 per barrel on Friday after rising in the previous session, as President Donald Trump stated that the US no longer needs a deal with Iran, dampening hopes for a diplomatic resolution to the war. Hopes for a faster resolution to the conflict have also been supported by a Democratic victory in the US midterm elections.

Venezuela Economic Growth Accelerates in Q3 — Venezuela’s economy expanded by 7.42% year-on-year in the third quarter of 2026, accelerating from a 7.14% growth in the previous period. This marked the 22nd consecutive quarter of economic expansion and the strongest in a year. Growth was primarily driven by non-oil activity, which increased 6.28%. Construction remained the strongest-performing sector, surging 35.75%, supported by investment to repair infrastructure damaged by the twin earthquakes at the end of the second quarter. Financial and insurance activities rose 29.29%, while agriculture expanded 7.13%, accommodation and food services (tourism) grew 8.95%, and manufacturing advanced 4.32%. The private sector as a whole recorded solid growth of 8.02%. Meanwhile, gross fixed capital formation increased 28.25%, pointing to stronger investment and confidence in the domestic productive sector.

【美國白宮將CFTC與SEC人選提交參議院審批】週四，白宮將針對美國商品期貨交易委員會（CFTC）和美國證券交易委員會（SEC）的兩黨提名名單正式提交給參議院，等待批准。此舉正值上週中期選舉失利後，總統唐納德·特朗普所在的共和黨即將於明年1月失去參議院控制權之際。民主黨人克里斯汀·約翰遜（Kristin Johnson）被提名為SEC委員，其任期將於2029年屆滿。她此前曾擔任CFTC委員，由時任民主黨總統喬·拜登任命。週四公佈的另一位SEC提名人是共和黨人史蒂文·萊文（Steven Levine），他將出任該機構委員，任期至2030年屆滿。民主黨人馬修·羅蘭（Matthew Rowland）被提名為CFTC委員，以填補剩餘任期至2028年屆滿的空缺。共和黨人內森·阿諾尼克（Nathan Anonick）同樣被提名為CFTC委員，其任期將於2031年屆滿。由特朗普挑選並於2025年獲參議院確認的CFTC主席邁克·塞利格（Mike Selig）在社交平臺X上發帖稱，羅蘭和阿諾尼克是“兩位強有力的跨黨派人選”，並表示期待與他們共事。

### 2026-11-13 07:36:44  #公司

【英偉達臨時CEO科萊特·克雷斯曾試圖管控公司最高風險的業務押注】據報道，英偉達於週四任命其資深首席財務官科萊特·克雷斯（Colette Kress）為臨時CEO，這位財務老將將被迫帶領公司度過因前任CEO黃仁勳突然辭職而引發的充滿不確定性的動盪風暴。現年59歲的克雷斯於2013年加入英偉達，當時該公司還只是一家默默無聞的遊戲晶片供應商。儘管她在引導公司崛起並在AI經濟中佔據核心地位的過程中發揮了重要作用，但據知情人士透露，她並不是公司重大決策的拍板人。黃仁勳對英偉達擁有絕對控制權，且從未制定過明確的繼任計劃。一位瞭解克雷斯的人士表示，每當兩人產生分歧時，黃仁勳往往會“強行壓制”她的意見。儘管如此，克雷斯在投資界依然廣為人知。她是除黃仁勳之外，唯一一位定期在英偉達季度財報電話會議上發言的高管。下一次財報電話會議定於下週二舉行。與黃仁勳相比，克雷斯可能會採取更為保守的策略。作為財務主管，她有時會對黃仁勳力推的交易提出反對意見。據知情人士透露，其中包括一項提議由英偉達為OpenAI提供高達250億美元信用擔保的交易，該交易旨在讓OpenAI利用英偉達最新一代晶片填滿8吉瓦的資料中心空間。今年8月，英偉達最終將該交易一分為二，目前僅承諾為第一部分提供約100億美元的信用擔保。據上述人士透露，近幾個月來，由於資料中心專案延期以及雲端計算交易取消，AI熱潮的圖景變得愈發複雜，克雷斯因此將工作重心放在尋找應對之策，以確保英偉達的財務風險不會過度集中在少數幾家公司身上。在加入英偉達之前，克雷斯曾擔任思科（Cisco）一個業務部門的首席財務官長達三年，並在微軟（Microsoft）工作了十餘年。

### 2026-11-13 07:19:57  #公司 #國際

【Anthropic與OpenAI被控抄襲神經網路專利】週四，一家由電腦科學家創立的人工智慧公司提起訴訟，指控Anthropic PBC和OpenAI OpCo涉嫌侵犯專利權。Flinn人工智慧技術有限責任公司（Flinn AI）向美國特拉華州地區法院提交了兩份訴狀，指控Anthropic和OpenAI均侵犯了由聯合發明人史蒂文·弗林（Steven Flinn）和娜奧米·曼尼佩尼（Naomi Moneypenny）獲得的五項專利。訴狀稱，這些發明可追溯至2015年末和2016年初，當時弗林與聯合發明人曼尼佩尼提交了專利申請，其“旨在保護這一代全新的基於注意力機制的神經網路技術，而該技術如今構成了現代生成式人工智慧系統的重要功能基礎”。針對Anthropic的訴訟主要聚焦於Claude系列大語言模型。訴狀稱，隨著該公司不斷推出更復雜的迭代版本，其侵權行為一直在持續。Flinn AI在訴狀中指出：“隨著Claude模型家族及其相關產品的每一次迭代與擴充套件，Anthropic的系統提供了更先進的基於注意力機制的推理、檢索、記憶、智慧體（Agentic）以及多模態功能，這些功能均侵犯了涉案專利。”針對OpenAI的訴狀中也包含了類似的段落，指控其隨著模型日益先進，侵權行為同樣在不斷升級。此外，弗林還創立了ManyWorlds Inc.公司，曼尼佩尼隨後也加入了該公司。今年早些時候，另一家名稱相似的公司Many Worlds 2T Innovations LLC曾起訴OpenAI和Spotify，指控它們侵犯了弗林與曼尼佩尼名下另一組與內容推薦相關的專利。

### 2026-11-13 07:11:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Peru #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Peru Raises Rate for First Time in Almost 4 Years — Peru’s Central Reserve Bank (BCRP) raised its benchmark interest rate by 25 bps to 4.50% in November 2026, marking its first hike in nearly four years, in line with expectations. The move reflected supply shocks, higher inflation expectations and strong domestic demand. Annual inflation accelerated to 5.3% in October from 4.5% in September, mainly due to higher food and energy prices. Inflation excluding food and energy rose to 4.6% from 4.5%, while the measure excluding food, energy and transportation stood at 2.0%, within the 1%-3% target range. Twelve-month inflation expectations remained at 3.1%, above the target ceiling. The BCRP expects inflation to return to target and converge toward 2% as supply shocks fade, but warned that El Niño and Middle East tensions could prolong price pressures. Activity indicators remained robust in October, while business expectations slowed but stayed optimistic. The bank said the hike does not necessarily mark a tightening cycle.

### 2026-11-13 07:00:30  #其他

【經濟日報金觀平：延伸產業鏈實現算力“價值輸出”】文章指出，我國發展算力產業擁有明顯優勢。一是規模優勢。二是能源優勢。三是效能優勢。也要看到，部分地區算力建設仍存在一些問題。比如，有的地方把算力產業簡單看作“建機房、上機架、買伺服器”，重建設輕運營、重硬體輕生態，算力建得快、用得少。把算力資源轉化為發展優勢，必須積極延伸產業鏈，實現算力的“價值輸出”。當前，我國區域發展不平衡問題依然突出，西部地區迫切需要找到能夠突破地理和自然條件約束、融入全球高階產業分工的發展新路徑。算力建設為打破傳統產業分工格局、縮小東西部發展差距提供了重要契機。

NZX 50 Retreats from Record High but Poised for 3rd Weekly Gain — The NZX 50 dropped 80 points, or 0.6%, to 14,179 in Friday morning trade, tracking a downbeat session on Wall Street overnight amid a plunge in Nvidia shares following the resignation of CEO Huang, while rising oil prices lifted Treasury yields. Traders also took profit after the broader index hit a record high a day earlier, supported by strong earnings reports from heavyweight stocks. Fresh data also pressured sentiment, as growth in New Zealand's manufacturing sector eased in October amid rising fuel prices, the cost of living, and concerns over election uncertainty. Rising oil prices further pressured the index, as inflation concerns and expectations of interest rate hikes increased. Almost all sectors traded in the red, led by industrials, with notable losers including Auckland International Airport (-3.4%), Turners Automotive (-1.9%), Fletcher Building (-1.6%), and Brisco Group (-1.2%). However, the index was up 1.3% for the week, marking its third straight weekly gain.

【騰訊謀變 馬化騰：AI大浪已打來，不能還坐著欣賞】11月11日，騰訊迎來成立28週年。按照慣例，這一天是騰訊的“Linktime”日，董事會主席馬化騰攜核心高管與全體員工線上對話，覆盤得失、校準方向。與往年不同的是，今年的這場直播馬化騰提示風險，要求員工適應AI變化。這次對話很快被傳成騰訊要裁員50%。11月12日，接近騰訊的知情人士表示，引發外界誤讀的，是管理層用來解釋資源排程的一組比喻。知情人士還原，在騰訊28週年的Linktime線上直播中，騰訊管理層主動表達了要加快組織變革的決心，同時特別提到，這和幾年前那次“降本增效”不一樣。前一次相對被動，更偏“降本”，這一次是面向AI時代的主動出擊，更偏“增效”，激發團隊去創造新的價值，鼓勵一線員工積極舉手向前衝，中間的管理層則需要變得更扁平和靈敏。為了應對新的變革，內部需要主動思考新打法和新戰場，50%的人像陸軍一樣，守土有責，透過提效去做好現有的核心業務；另50%的人像海軍一樣，擁抱浪潮，探索AI Native、拓展新的發展空間。知情人士表示，這是公司資源適應AI時代的重新排程，可能是所謂“裁員50%”的謠言誤讀出處，該說法完全不屬實。騰訊內部人士透露，公司內部業務眾多，有些業務已經在AI上做得比較好，有些動作不大，管理層的意思是希望沒有動員起來的要動起來，不要等靠。“這是內部講話，是一個概述，傳遞一個發展方向，並不是發內部檔案。”騰訊內部人士表示。(證券時報)

### 2026-11-13 05:44:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #New Zealand #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

NZ Manufacturing PMI in Expansion for 16th Straight Month — New Zealand’s BusinessNZ Performance of Manufacturing Index eased to 54.9 in October 2026 from 56.3 in September, marking a 16-month streak of expansion. Respondents cited fuel prices, the cost of living, and election uncertainty as concerns, though many pointed to the Christmas build-up and steady orders as positive factors. Employment was the weakest sub-index at 51.9, though still expanding, while Deliveries (61.4) and Finished Stocks (58.0) were the strongest. New Orders (54.3) and Production (53.9) eased from the previous month but remained comfortably in expansion. The Christmas build-up and steady infrastructure work suggest manufacturers are ending the year on a solid footing, even as input prices and the cost of living continue to weigh on the sector.

### 2026-11-13 05:34:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Import Prices YoY #Importance 1

South Korea Import Prices Rise in October — South Korea’s import prices rose 13.5% year on year in October 2026, following a 17.8% increase in September. Gains were led by raw materials, which climbed 36.2%, while intermediate goods prices rose 9.2%. In contrast, capital goods prices fell 4.2% and consumer goods prices declined 3.3%. By economic sector, mining product prices surged 40.7%, driven by a 47.6% jump in coal, crude petroleum and natural gas amid elevated international crude oil and gas prices caused by supply disruptions from the US-Iran war. Manufactured goods prices rose 4.8%, led by a 52.5% increase in coal and petroleum products. Meanwhile, prices of agricultural, forestry, and marine products fell 1.9%, weighed down by an 8% drop in livestock products. The Net Barter Terms of Trade Index increased 21.3%, while the Import Price Index decreased 1.8% from the previous month.

### 2026-11-13 05:27:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Korea #Export Prices YoY #Importance 1

South Korea Export Prices Rise in October — South Korea’s export prices surged 36.7% year on year in October 2026, easing from an upwardly revised 41.3% increase in September. Prices of manufactured goods rose 36.7%, led by a 95.9% jump in computers, electronic, and optical equipment. Coal and petroleum product prices also surged 77.2%, while prices of agricultural, forestry, and marine products rose 25.4%. The Export Volume Index increased 14.9% and the Export Value Index rose 36.6%. Meanwhile, the Export Price Index edged up 0.7% from the previous month.

### 2026-11-13 05:24:55  #MKT News #Star Stocks #NVIDIA #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #NVDA.O #Impact bearish #Nasdaq100

【NVIDIA interim CEO may pursue more cautious strategy than Jensen Huang】The Information reports interim CEO and CFO Colette Kress has repeatedly objected to high‑risk deals pushed by Jensen Huang and may pursue a more conservative operating strategy. Kress opposed a prior plan for NVIDIA to provide up to $250 bln in credit support to OpenAI; that package was split and NVIDIA now commits about $100 bln. After data center project delays and cancelled cloud deals, Kress has moved to reduce NVIDIA’s concentration risk—the company has already provided more than $100 bln in investments and credit guarantees to customers and supply‑chain partners. Some investors fear financing support could be tightened after Huang’s departure; one cloud customer is reportedly weighing a switch to Google or AMD chips. Kress must also navigate customer procurement diversification, tighter U.S. export controls and internal power realignments; she has previously clashed with Huang and his family on company matters.

### 2026-11-13 04:51:18  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #S&P500 #Impact bearish

【DALY: Further hikes may be needed to curb inf】San Francisco Fed President DALY said further Fed tightening may be necessary to reduce inf, noting persistent price pressures that are not confined to industries directly affected by tariffs or oil. She said an increasing number of firms report plans to pass higher input costs to consumers and that pricing willingness is strengthening. DALY said she supports a September rate hike and holding policy unchanged in October. On AI, she said AI infrastructure is raising prices in some areas but its overall effect on inf is so far modest and not broad-based; she expects AI to boost productivity over the long run but the timing is uncertain. DALY is not an FOMC voter this year.

【US October fiscal deficit $404bn, up 42% YoY】The US Treasury said the first month of fiscal 2027 (Oct 2026) posted a $404bn deficit, up 42% YoY. Outlays rose 12% YoY to $773bn, driven by higher military, healthcare and net interest spending. Receipts fell 9% to $369bn; tariff revenue dropped about 39% to $19.2bn and larger tariff and corporate tax refunds also weighed on receipts. Treasury projects the fiscal-year deficit will widen modestly to roughly $2.2trn, versus $1.99trn a year earlier (about 6% of GDP).

### 2026-11-13 04:33:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Currency #Importance 1

Brazilian Real Extends Losses on Fiscal Concerns — The Brazilian real weakened to over 5.65 per USD in November, extending its lowest since since May of last year, as the government’s fiscal adjustment bill disappointed markets. Markets had previously welcomed the news as an initial step toward a more robust fiscal agenda, but now view the bill as a timid adjustment. The measures are understood not to focus on structural adjustments to reverse the growing public debt trajectory, but rather on opening more room for spending freezes to reach the 0.5% of GDP surplus target for 2027. The government has faced market pressure to announce fiscal adjustments after Brazilian assets plunged following President Lula’s reelection. Meanwhile, annual inflation rose to 4.63% in October from 4.58% in September, below market expectations of 4.74% but marking a four-month high and remaining above the BCB’s 4.5% target ceiling. With inflation still above target, the central bank is expected to remain cautious about resuming its Selic easing cycle.

Brent Crude Rises on Uncertainty Over US-Iran Diplomacy — Brent crude rose to around $96 a barrel on Thursday, extending gains for a second consecutive session and rebounding from a ten-week low of $91.5 reached on November 10th. Prices climbed after US President Trump declared that the US no longer needs a deal with Iran. The remarks marked a sharp departure from Monday, when Trump described the war as "militarily effectively over" following talks with Qatar's prime minister. Meanwhile, Iran has shown little willingness to soften its negotiating position. Mohsen Rezaee, secretary of Iran's Supreme National Security Council, said Tehran would not remove any enriched nuclear material from the country. Despite the diplomatic uncertainty, Middle Eastern crude supplies have recovered considerably. Saudi Arabia reported a 26% increase in October production, while Iraq also raised output. However, shipments remain dependent on alternative routes, ship-to-ship transfers and military protection.

US Stocks Fall on Nvidia Crisis — US stock indices moved lower on Thursday as Nvidia’s leadership crisis curbed risk appetite and an oil price rally lifted Treasury yields. The S&P 500 and the Nasdaq each edged about 0.3% lower, while the Dow shed over 50 points. Nvidia tumbled 6% following the ouster of CEO Huang. The US is investigating Alice Huang, whom officials suspect may be a spy, over whether entities linked to her were involved in efforts to smuggle products to China. The chip sector was mixed, Micron fell nearly 2.5% and Intel lost over 1%, while Broadcom and AMD gained over 5% each. Rising oil prices also weighed on sentiment, reviving energy inflation concerns. Bond yields climbed on expectations that the Federal Reserve will step up the pace of rate hikes to cool inflation, leaving credit-sensitive stocks mostly lower. Meanwhile, Alphabet rose over 2% on its investment in TPU production.

【克利夫蘭聯儲行長稱通脹正朝錯誤方向發展 利率需更具限制性】克利夫蘭聯儲行長貝絲·哈馬克重申，她認為在人工智慧投資所帶來的需求壓力下，需要提高利率以抗擊通脹。“我的預期是我們需要將政策轉向限制性立場，以幫助通脹回落至2%的目標，這一點至關重要，”哈馬克週四在俄亥俄州哥倫布的活動上表示。“如果我們不這樣做，我擔心通脹思維可能開始紮根。”哈馬克表示，面對資料中心建設帶來的需求壓力，美聯儲有充分的應對能力。“當我權衡這些因素時，我看到的是通脹一直很頑固，而且持續時間比我預期的要長，”她說。剔除食品和能源價格的基礎通脹10月升幅超過預期。哈馬克表示，這份報告顯示，通脹方面的進展“確實停滯”。“我看到的最新資料顯示通脹正朝錯誤的方向發展。我們正在遠離2%的目標，而不是向這個目標靠攏，”她說。上月美聯儲決定將利率維持在3.75%-4%的區間不變，哈馬克是兩名持異議的官員之一。當時她傾向於加息25個基點。

### 2026-11-13 03:28:44  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Inflation Rate MoM #Importance 1

Argentina Monthly Inflation Rate Hits Six-Month High — Argentina's monthly inflation rate rose to 2.2% in October 2026 from 1.9% in the prior month, marking a six-month high. Utilities recorded the largest price increase (4.2%), driven by higher gas prices. Recreation and culture followed, up 3.3%. Food and non-alcoholic beverages contributed the most to the monthly variation across most regions, rising 2% nationally. Prices also rose considerably for clothing and footwear (2.2%), healthcare (1.7%), transport (2.1%), communication (2%), and restaurants and hotels (1.7%). Seasonal prices rose 5.5% and regulated prices increased 2.9%. Core inflation eased to 1.5% from 1.7% in September. Year on year, Argentina's inflation eased slightly to 33.1% from 33.4%, missing estimates of 32.7%.

US Budget Deficit Widens More Than Forecast in October — The US government budget deficit widened to $404 billion in October 2026 from $284 billion a year earlier, missing forecasts of a $298.6 billion gap. Outlays rose to $773 billion from $689 billion. The largest expenditures were Medicare ($170 billion), Social Security ($143 billion), national defense ($103 billion), and net interest ($103 billion), followed by health ($94 billion), income security ($58 billion), and veterans' benefits ($54 billion). Meanwhile, receipts declined to $369 billion from $404 billion, with individual income taxes ($186 billion), social insurance and retirement ($131 billion), and customs duties ($19 billion) as the largest revenue sources.

【智利上調鋰產量預期，市場明年料重返過剩】智利上調了今年的鋰產量預期，儘管需求增長放緩和供應上升預計將推動全球市場在2027年重返過剩狀態。智利國家銅業委員會（Cochilco）週四表示，全球第三大鋰生產國智利在2026年將生產約32.5萬噸碳酸鋰當量（LCE），高於一年前預測的30.5萬噸。2027年產量預計將升至33.5萬噸。受電池儲能系統快速增長的推動，全球鋰需求今年預計將增長25%，達到208萬噸，較Cochilco一年前的預測高出約34%。但需求增長預計在2027年將大幅放緩至8%，屆時消費量將達到225萬噸。Cochilco預計今年全球將出現7.9萬噸的供應短缺，而一年前預測的是6萬噸的供應過剩。到2027年，隨著新供應逐步入市，供應增速預計將超過需求，推動市場重返4.2萬噸的過剩狀態。

### 2026-11-13 02:56:47  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #DXY #Impact bullish #US10Y #Impact bearish #S&P500

【Chicago Fed's Goolsbee: US inf at 'dangerous moment'; data-center demand a watchpoint】Chicago Fed President Goolsbee said US inflation is at a "dangerous moment" after October CPI surprised to the upside, marking a second consecutive monthly increase. He had expected inflation to ease as tariff effects faded and oil fell, but that has not yet occurred. Goolsbee flagged persistent price pressure in services and healthcare that he says is unrelated to tariffs or oil. The Fed is trying to separate supply-side shocks (tariffs, prolonged high oil) from demand-side overheating tied to large-scale data-center construction. AI-related inflation remains concentrated in AI, tech and related sectors and has not yet broadened; the Fed is monitoring for spread. He warned that if data-center driven demand overheating starts to affect the wider economy it would become a problem. Proposals by newly elected Democratic governors to pause data-center builds are not directly connected to the Fed's inflation work.

TSX Falls on Miners and Banks Losses — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell about 0.5% to hover below 36,500 on Thursday amid losses in the banking and mining sectors. Miners posted losses as gold prices fell sharply, pressured by a stronger US dollar, expectations of further Fed tightening, and rising oil prices. Agnico Eagle lost nearly 3%, while Barrick and WPM shed over 1% each. Franco-Nevada edged lower after signaling its annual earnings would land at the midpoint of earlier guidance rather than the top half. Meanwhile, banks declined as rebounding oil prices amid escalating US-Iran tensions revived inflation fears and reinforced expectations of a restrictive monetary policy stance from both the BoC and the Fed. Scotiabank, TD Bank, and BMO lost near 0.5% each. In technology, Shopify fell over 0.5% amid the Nvidia crisis, which increased uncertainty in the sector. Celestica gained nearly 5.5%, extending an 8.4% surge from the prior session driven by recent investor-day commentary and strong third-quarter results.

Gasoline Futures Rise — US gasoline futures edged up to $2.64 per gallon but remained close to an eight-month low, as traders weighed Middle East tensions against recovering inventories. EIA data showed gasoline stocks rose by 1.8 million barrels in the week ending November 6, lifting domestic supplies to 1% above seasonal norms for the first time since April. Seasonal declines in driving demand ahead of winter and near-capacity refinery operations have supported the recovery. Still, uncertainty over a prolonged US-Iran conflict remains an upside risk. President Trump has downplayed the need for a deal with Iran, further dampening hopes of an imminent resolution, although crude continues to flow through the waterway. A shift by US refiners away from gasoline towards higher-margin middle distillates, alongside seasonal refinery maintenance, could further constrain supply. Escalating Ukrainian attacks on Russian refineries and lower Chinese exports are also contributing to tighter global fuel markets.

Lumber Slides Further as Housing Weakness Persists — Lumber futures fell below $490 per thousand board feet, reaching their lowest level since July 2024, amid persistent weakness in the US housing market. Existing-home sales fell to their lowest level in 16 years, as mortgage rates above 7% and stubbornly high inflation continued to deter buyers. Although mortgage rates declined for a fourth consecutive week to 7.11%, affordability remains strained. Construction activity is also weak, with spending on lumber-intensive single-family homes falling 2.4% year-on-year in September, while builder sentiment dropped to its lowest level since 2012 amid high input costs. Mill closures across North America could tighten supply as prolonged US-Canada tariff uncertainty remains a headwind. A Democratic sweep of Congress has raised prospects for repealing duties on Canadian lumber, although the Trump administration has yet to secure a trade agreement amid ongoing negotiations.

European Stocks Inch Higher — European stocks closed mixed on Thursday amid a batch of major corporate earnings results, while the ousting of Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang halted the recent rally for AI companies. The Euro STOXX 50 rose 0.1% to 6,399 and the STOXX Europe 600 extended its record high by 0.1% to 664. Siemens added 1.7% after it raised its medium-term targets on record order levels, largely due to higher demand for data center products and industrial factory automation. The insurance sector was also in the spotlight, with Allianz adding 2% after posting strong profits and raising its 2026 outlook, while Munich Re raised its profit guidance and jumped 2.9%. On the other hand, Enel lost 1.5% after its results. ASML fell 2% to pare its surge from earlier in the week, tracking the North American AI infrastructure sector following the leadership shake-up at Nvidia.

親愛的NVIDIA員工：

我寫這封信是為了分享一項重要人事變動。黃仁勳已辭去執行長及董事會成員職務，董事會已要求首席財務官科萊特·克雷斯出任臨時執行長，同時啟動永久繼任者的遴選工作，範圍涵蓋內部和外部候選人。我將出任公司首位董事會主席。

科萊特擔任首席財務官超過13年，是NVIDIA增長與資本戰略的關鍵設計者之一。她對公司業務及我們所處的市場有著深刻理解。董事會完全相信，科萊特能夠兌現我們對股東、客戶、合作伙伴以及每一位員工的承諾。

黃仁勳對NVIDIA的貢獻意義深遠。NVIDIA是全球最具影響力的科技公司之一。我們開創了加速計算，定義了現代GPU，並打造了驅動人工智慧的平臺。

董事會認真對待自身作為這家偉大公司守護者的職責。

公司的實力源於我們的員工、領導團隊、技術實力和合作伙伴生態。我相信，在科萊特的領導下，在董事會的全力支援下，我們將繼續推動業務增長，引領行業向前發展。感謝各位的專注、專業精神以及對NVIDIA的投入。

哈維·瓊斯
NVIDIA董事會主席

Brent Crude Rises from 10-Week Low — Brent crude rose above $94 a barrel on Thursday, extending gains for a second consecutive session as prices attempted to recover from a ten-week low of $91.5 on November 10. Investors weighed escalating tensions between Washington and Tehran against signs of recovering supply from Saudi Arabia. President Donald Trump said the US no longer needs a deal with Iran, signalling a shift away from recent diplomatic efforts. Tehran also maintained a hardline position, with senior official Mohsen Rezaee ruling out the removal of any enriched nuclear material from the country. Still, Saudi Arabia reported a 26% increase in oil production in October, bringing output to one of its highest monthly levels in years, while Iraq also raised production to a wartime record. ### 2026-11-13 01:11:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Crude Oil Stocks Change #Importance 1

US Crude Inventories Rise the Most in 3 Months — US crude oil inventories increased by 4.657 million barrels in the week ended November 6, marking the largest build since August and reversing five consecutive weeks of declines. Markets anticipated a 1.1 million-barrel draw. Stocks at the Cushing, Oklahoma, delivery hub rose by 106,000 barrels, while refinery crude processing increased by 322,000 barrels per day. Refined product inventories also recorded gains, with distillate stocks, including diesel and heating oil, climbing by 4.071 million barrels, the largest increase since July. Gasoline inventories rose by a further 1.799 million barrels. Meanwhile, net US crude imports increased by 544,000 barrels per day.

US 30-Year Mortgage Rate Hits 7-Week Low — The average rate on a 30-year fixed-rate mortgage in the US fell to 7.11% as of November 12, 2026, down from 7.18% the previous week. This marks the fourth consecutive decline and a seven-week low. “Mortgage rates dropped to their lowest level in seven weeks, a welcome development that gives borrowers greater flexibility as they navigate the current housing market,” said Sam Khater, Freddie Mac's Chief Economist. A year ago, the 30-year fixed-rate mortgage averaged 6.24%. Meanwhile, the average rate on a 15-year fixed-rate mortgage also declined to 6.45%, down from 6.57% last week.

### 2026-11-13 01:03:47  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cocoa Futures Cross $5,200 — Cocoa futures rose above $5,200 a tonne, pulling away from four-month lows hit on November 10, as supply concerns in top grower Ivory Coast intensified. The country's national weather agency (Sodexam) warned that cocoa production could fall between 21% and 25% in the 2026/27 season due to an exceptionally strong El Niño event. Annual rainfall is expected to decline by 10%–27%, shortening the rainy season and extending dry periods. Near-term supply flows are also facing disruption, as heavy rains have reportedly damaged roads in Ivory Coast, hampering bean deliveries to collection centres. Port arrivals remain well below year-ago levels, although dealers suggested that increased smuggling into neighbouring countries may partly explain the decline. However, subdued chocolate demand has contributed to a build-up in exchange inventories. ICE-monitored cocoa stocks have been rising since the start of the year, recently reaching a fresh 2.5-year high of 3,751,670 bags.

US Stock Indices Pare Losses — US stock indices pared losses on Thursday as markets assessed the AI infrastructure landscape after Jensen Huang resigned as CEO of Nvidia. The S&P 500 and the Nasdaq 100 inched above the flatline, while the Dow added over 100 points. Nvidia was down more than 5% after losing up to 14% earlier in the session as CFO Colette Kress was named interim CEO following Huang's resignation over his involvement with a suspected Chinese spy. Huang's stewardship saw Nvidia's participation in and orchestration of massive circular investments across all tiers of the AI sector, implicating global financial markets in the leadership change. Still, competitors AMD and Broadcom over 5%, while Alphabet's investment in TPU production drove its shares up near 3%. Meanwhile, Nebius sank nearly 16% after reporting delays in some data center capacity buildouts. Other sectors rebounded from Tuesday's selloff, which had been triggered by a hot CPI reading that bolstered bets on more Fed rate hikes next year.

### 2026-11-13 00:31:41  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

US 10-Year Yield Surges — The yield on the 10-year US Treasury note rose above 5.05% on Thursday, extending its recent increase to a three-week high on expectations that the Federal Reserve will deliver multiple rate hikes in the current tightening cycle. CPI data released earlier in the week showed that both headline and core consumer prices rose more than expected in October, indicating that the surge in energy prices from the war in Iran was being transmitted to other sectors of the economy. Markets consequently piled into rate futures pricing in a Fed rate hike in December and two similar moves next year. Meanwhile, Treasury yields in the belly of the curve surged after Jensen Huang resigned as president and CEO of Nvidia, sparking fears of turmoil in the debt-laden AI sector. Because Nvidia has anchored and backstopped a massive wave of AI-related corporate bond issuance, a sudden freeze in the sector's credit activity risked triggering broader fixed-income selloffs as investors raised cash.

### 2026-11-13 00:01:23  #央行 #國際

【印尼人民幣清算行正式開業】印度尼西亞人民幣清算行12日在印尼首都雅加達正式開業，中國銀行擔任印尼人民幣清算行。來自中印尼兩國政府部門及企業、金融機構的約100名代表參加當日在印尼央行舉行的開業儀式。 目前，包括中國銀行、中國工商銀行、中國建設銀行、曼底利銀行、印尼國家銀行等在內的12家本地和外國銀行作為特許交叉貨幣做市商，成為人民幣清算行的參加行，相關交易基礎設施均已準備就緒。 印尼央行行長德斯特里·達瑪揚蒂在致辭中表示，隨著人民幣清算行的啟用及本幣交易機制的完善，企業可直接透過人民幣進行對華貿易結算，有助於降低對第三方貨幣的依賴，維護匯率穩定並增強區域經濟應對外部衝擊的韌性。（新華社）

【超聚變創業板IPO過會】11月12日，深圳證券交易所上市稽核委員會召開2026年第61次審議會議。會議結果顯示，超聚變數字技術股份有限公司（簡稱“超聚變”）首發事項符合發行條件、上市條件和資訊披露要求。據悉，公司此次IPO募集資金重點投向新一代算力產品研發與關鍵核心技術攻關。超聚變成立於2021年，總部位於河南自貿試驗區鄭州片區，是一家算力基礎設施與數智化服務提供商。招股說明書（上會稿）顯示，其定位於企業在AI和資料時代的水平全棧解決方案提供者，在提供算力業務的同時，探索智企生態業務、能源智慧解決方案等新興業務領域。目前公司已穩居國內算力基礎設施第一梯隊。(上海證券報)

European Natural Gas Soars by 7% — European natural gas prices surged 7% to €92.70/MWh, driven by forecasts of colder weather, supply disruptions in Norway and persistent geopolitical tensions in the Middle East. Temperatures are expected to fall across Europe from November 17, with further cold spells possible in late November and early December, boosting heating demand. Meanwhile, Norwegian gas flows declined as outages intensified, particularly at the Troll field, where operator Gassco said the duration of the disruption remained uncertain. Supply concerns have been compounded by low storage levels: EU reserves stood below 72%, compared with a five-year seasonal average of 89%, while Germany’s stocks were below 60%. Geopolitical risks added further support after US President Donald Trump dismissed the need for a deal with Iran, while Tehran maintained a hardline stance on its enriched nuclear material.

Costa Rica GDP Growth Slows in Q3 2026 — Costa Rica’s economy expanded 2.7% year on year in Q3 2026, slowing from an upwardly revised 3.53% in the previous quarter and marking the weakest expansion since Q1 2021. Manufacturing output declined 3.7% after contracting 0.98% in Q2, and growth slowed across multiple sectors. Agriculture and fishing eased to 0.06% from 0.63%, while mining and quarrying edged down to 2.99% from 3.11%. Transport and warehousing fell to 2.06% from 3.11%, and accommodation and food services slowed to 2.83% from 3.6%. Financial activities and insurance eased to 4.71% from 5.82%, while growth in professional, scientific, technical, administrative and support services decelerated to 3.32% from 4.15%.

### 2026-11-12 23:06:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Existing Home Sales #Importance 3

US Existing Home Sales Lowest Since 2010 — Existing home sales in the US fell 5.5% to a seasonally adjusted annual rate of 3.75 million units in October 2026, the lowest level since August 2010 and below market expectations of 3.85 million. NAR Chief Economist Lawrence Yun attributed the weakness to elevated mortgage rates, which have reached their highest levels since 2000, excluding one month in 2023. He noted that homeowners remain financially secure due to accumulated housing wealth, while lower mortgage rates could unlock significant pent-up demand. Sales were unchanged in the Northeast at 470,000 units, while the Midwest declined 3.2% to 900,000, the South fell 6.5% to 1.73 million and the West dropped 9.7% to 650,000. Meanwhile, housing inventory rose 1.3% from September and 4.6% year over year to 1.59 million homes, equivalent to 5.1 months of supply, up from 4.7 months in September. The median home price increased 0.4% from a year earlier to $416,700.

Baltic Dry Index Advances for 7th Day — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, was up for a seventh straight session on Thursday, rising by 2.1% to its highest since October 5 at 3,020 points. Gains were broad-based across vessel segments, particularly the larger one. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, advanced by 2.9% to its highest since October 2 at 4,836 points. At the same time, the supramax index increased by 1.2% to an over one-week high of 1,831 points; and the panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, went up 0.7% to its highest level since October 22 at 2,117 points.

Silver Drops on Strong Dollar and Fed Rate Expectations — Silver prices fell around 3% to approximately $50 an ounce on Thursday, retreating to levels not seen in months as a stronger US dollar, rising oil prices and growing expectations of further Federal Reserve tightening weighed on the metal. Stronger-than-expected US consumer inflation data for October, released on Tuesday, pushed the dollar to a 19-month high and reinforced bets on further Fed rate hikes. Meanwhile, renewed gains in oil prices added to inflationary pressures after President Donald Trump said the US did not need a deal with Iran and redirected Vice President JD Vance towards domestic priorities rather than negotiations with Tehran. According to CME’s FedWatch Tool, markets are pricing in nearly a 90% probability of a 25-basis-point rate hike in December, while the likelihood of a larger 50-basis-point increase has climbed to around 10%, up from zero before the inflation data.

The first direct negotiations in months ended with an agreement on a prisoner exchange but no breakthrough on protecting maritime trade. ### 2026-11-12 22:39:44  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Currency #Importance 1

Brazilian Real Hits Lowest Since May — The Brazilian real weakened towards 5.62 per USD in November, its lowest since May of last year, amid prospects that a vote on the government’s fiscal measures will be postponed. Senate President Alcolumbre said that if any lawmaker needs more time to analyze the complementary fiscal adjustment bill, the discussion could be delayed. The bill combines measures to contain spending and increase revenue; if approved, it is projected to yield savings of R$50 billion to R$60 billion in 2027. The government has faced market pressure to announce fiscal adjustments after Brazilian assets plunged following President Lula’s late-October reelection. Meanwhile, annual inflation rose to 4.63% in October from 4.58% in September, below market expectations of 4.74% but marking a four-month high and remaining above the BCB’s 4.5% target ceiling. With 12-month inflation still above target, the central bank is expected to remain cautious about resuming its Selic easing cycle.

Nvidia shares sank 14% after CFO Colette Kress was named interim CEO following a board meeting that resulted in Huang's resignation, due to his involvement with a Megaspeed executive widely suspected of being a Chinese spy. Huang's stewardship of Nvidia saw the company grow to a market cap of nearly $6 trillion and led deal-making across the entire AI supply chain, implicating the global tech sector in the leadership change. AMD and Broadcom were higher, although AI infrastructure stocks sank sharply. Meanwhile, Nebius sank nearly 10% after delaying some data center capacity buildout. Still, other sectors offered some respite, rebounding after the hot CPI reading on Tuesday supported bets of more Federal Reserve rate hikes in the current tightening cycle.

Brent Crude Eases Gains — Brent crude rose to as high as $94.3 a barrel on Thursday before paring gains to $93 a barrel, nearing its lowest since August 31, as signs of recovering Saudi Arabian supply outweighed escalating tensions between Washington and Tehran. Meanwhile, President Donald Trump said the US no longer needs a deal with Iran, signalling a shift away from recent diplomatic efforts. Tehran also maintained a hardline position, with senior official Mohsen Rezaee ruling out the removal of any enriched nuclear material from the country.

### 2026-11-12 22:13:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Current Account #Importance 1

German Current Account Surplus Widens in September — Germany’s current account surplus increased to €18.7 billion in September 2026, up from €15.7 billion a year earlier. The primary income surplus rose to €16.0 billion from €13.6 billion, while the secondary income deficit narrowed to €5.3 billion from €7.1 billion. Meanwhile, the goods surplus edged down to €15.6 billion from €16.2 billion, as exports grew 10.4% year-on-year to €129.7 billion, while imports increased at a faster 12.7% to €114.1 billion. The services deficit also widened to €7.4 billion from €7.1 billion. For the first nine months of 2026, Germany’s cumulative current account surplus reached €160.0 billion, compared with €149.4 billion in the same period of 2025.

Canada Building Permits Fall in September — Canada’s building permits declined 4.1% to C$12.9 billion in September 2026 from August, following a downwardly revised 7.6% jump in the prior month and slightly missing forecasts of a 4% drop. Residential construction intentions fell by C$475.7 million to C$7.8 billion, as a C$529.3 million drop in the multi-family component was partly offset by a C$53.6 million rise in single-family permits. Multi-family declines were led by British Columbia (-C$292.1 million) and Alberta (-C$186.8 million). Meanwhile, non-residential permits fell C$77.1 million to C$5.1 billion, as a C$171.5 million drop in institutional permits was moderated by a C$90.9 million gain in industrial permits. Institutional declines were led by Quebec (-C$95.5 million), Saskatchewan (-C$43.6 million), and Alberta (-C$39.3 million). In Q3 2026, total building permit value decreased 3.6% quarter over quarter to C$38.8 billion.

### 2026-11-12 21:18:29  #Trading Economics #Economy #Romania #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Romania Holds Interest Rate in November — The National Bank of Romania kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 6.50% on November 12, as expected, maintaining the Lombard lending rate at 7.50% and the deposit facility rate at 5.50%. The decision comes amid persistent inflationary pressures, with annual inflation rising to 6.94% in October from 6.17% in August, driven largely by higher fuel and energy prices. The central bank expects inflation to increase slightly further towards the end of 2026 before gradually declining, returning to its target range in the first quarter of 2028. Meanwhile, economic activity is expected to recover moderately in the second half of 2026, although fiscal consolidation, weaker consumption and the Middle East conflict pose risks to growth. The NBR also highlighted uncertainty surrounding energy and oil prices, exchange-rate developments and global financial conditions. The bank reaffirmed that maintaining price stability remains its priority amid elevated economic uncertainty.

### 2026-11-12 20:14:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Brazil Inflation Rate Rises Less Than Expected — Brazil’s annual inflation rose to 4.63% in October from 4.58% in September, below market expectations of an increase to 4.74%, marking a second consecutive monthly increase and the highest rate in four months. The acceleration was driven by higher price growth in food and beverages, which increased to 5.73% from 4.66% in September. Inflation also accelerated for household articles (2.88% vs. 2.08%) and communication (3.68% vs. 2.79%), while inflation for education edged up slightly to 5.96% from 5.95%. In contrast, inflation slowed for transport to 3.2% from 3.94% as fuel inflation softened to 4.3% from 4.97%. Price growth also slowed for housing (4.01% vs 4.22%), clothing (3.21% vs 3.34%), health and personal care (5.42% vs 5.75%) and personal expenses (5.8% vs 5.9%). From the previous month, consumer prices rose 0.14% following a 0.82% rise in September, also below market expectations. As of October, inflation reached 4.1%.

### 2026-11-12 19:56:13  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #CSI300 #Impact bearish

【Multiple public REITs issue secondary-market volatility warnings】Secondary-market volatility in public REITs has risen. As of Nov 12, the CSI REITs total‑return index is down 7.36% YTD and the CSI REITs price index is down 11.73% YTD. On a monthly basis only January posted a gain (+3.98%); REITs have recorded nine consecutive monthly declines. Seventeen public REITs are positive YTD — China Merchants Expressway REIT, CICC Shandong Expressway REIT and ChinaAMC China Communications Construction REIT are up >10% — while 61 are negative YTD; 21 are down >20%, 10 down >30% and 3 down >40%, with industrial‑park and warehousing/logistics names most affected. Huatai Securities fixed‑income research says October’s core tension was improved expectations for incremental funding versus limited actual buying, producing a pattern of steady‑then‑weak, slow rises and fast falls: month‑start demand expansion plus falling rates supported allocations to cash‑flow‑stable, high‑distribution assets (eg guaranteed‑rental housing), while month‑end renewed trading supply, a rate uptick and five concentrated new REIT listings amplified price moves. Huatai adds deeply discounted industrial‑park and logistics REITs may present trading opportunities, but only after unlocked shares fully turnover, volumes ease and selling pressure is absorbed.

### 2026-11-12 19:56:08  #公司 #市場 #觀點

【多隻公募REITs釋出二級市場波動風險提示】近期，公募REITs二級市場波動加劇，截至11月12日，中證REITs全收益指數年內下跌7.36%，中證REITs指數同期下跌11.73%。單月來看，除今年1月上漲3.98%外，月線已實現9連跌。具體來看，目前有17只公募REITs年內錄得正漲幅，招商高速公路REIT、中金山東高速REIT、華夏中國交建REIT年內漲幅超10%；61只公募REITs年內收跌，21只跌幅超過20%，10只跌幅超過30%，更有3只跌幅超過40%，產業園、倉儲物流類成為“重災區”。華泰證券固收研究表示，10月公募REITs的核心矛盾是增量資金預期改善，但實際承接力量仍然有限，市場呈現前穩後弱、慢漲快跌的特徵。“月初需求擴容預期疊加利率下行，配置型資金增配現金流穩定、分派率保護較強的資產，如保租房等；月末市場重新交易供給壓力，利率上行和5只新REITs集中上市，少量減持便容易放大價格波動。”不過，華泰證券固收研究認為，產業園和倉儲物流中的深度折價品種可能具備交易機會，但需等待解禁份額充分換手、成交量回落和拋壓消化。（中國基金報）

### 2026-11-12 19:44:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #Tanzania #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Tanzania Inflation Rate Eases to 4.2% in October — The annual inflation rate in Tanzania edged down to 4.2% in October 2026 from 4.3% in each of the previous two months. Price growth cooled down mostly for food & non-alcoholic beverages (3.6% vs 3.9% in September); clothing & footwear (1.2% vs 1.7%); housing & utilities (2.3% vs 2.5%) and furnishings & household equipment (1.8% vs 2.6%). On the other hand, prices rose faster for transportation (14.9% vs 14%) and miscellaneous goods & services (3.5% vs 3.3%). On a monthly basis, the CPI fell by 0.3%, after increasing by 0.1% in the month earlier.
